

asoiaf_destroy_crownlands_title_effect = {
	destroy_title = title:e_the_crownlands
}
asoiaf_destroy_ww_ok_effect = {
	root = {
		every_equipped_character_artifact = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = valyrian_steel
				NOT = { has_variable = asoiaf_is_ice }
			}
			save_scope_as = this_artifact
		}
		destroy_artifact = scope:this_artifact
	}
}

asoiaf_religion_doctrines_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_game_rule = default_gender_equality }
		faith:old_gods_wnw = {
			remove_doctrine = doctrine_gender_equal
			add_doctrine = doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		}
		faith:valyrian_pan_freehold = {
			remove_doctrine = doctrine_gender_equal
			add_doctrine = doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		}
		faith:fots_dragon = {
			remove_doctrine = doctrine_gender_equal
			add_doctrine = doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		}
	}
	faith:old_gods_wnw = {
		remove_doctrine = doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		add_doctrine = doctrine_consanguinity_aunt_nephew_and_uncle_niece
	}
	faith:rhllor_fots = {
		remove_doctrine = doctrine_pluralism_righteous
		add_doctrine = doctrine_pluralism_fundamentalist
	}
}

asoiaf_setup_houses_effect = {
	character:Baelish_2 ?= {
		house = { save_scope_as = asoiaf_founders_old_house }
		set_house = house:house_BaelishHH
		dynasty = {
			every_dynasty_member = {
				limit = {
					any_ancestor = {
						this = character:Baelish_2
					}
					house = house:asoiaf_founders_old_house
				}
				set_house = house:house_BaelishHH
				trigger_event = { id = agot_coa_events.1 }
			}
		}
		add_to_global_variable_list = {
			name = asoiaf_baelish_hh_unavailable
			target = flag:asoiaf_baelish_cadet_hh
		}
	}
	character:Ryswell_3 ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
				NOT = { has_character_flag = has_personal_coa }
			}
			agot_create_personal_coa_for_historical_ai = { ACTOR = character:Ryswell_3 COA_KEY = rodrick_ryswell_personal_coa }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_landed = yes
				has_character_flag = has_personal_coa
			}
			every_held_title = {
				limit = {
					agot_is_valid_dyn_coa_title = yes
				}
				set_coa = holder.var:my_personal_coa.house
			}
			add_character_flag = landed_coa_personalized
		}
	}
	character:Ryswell_4 ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
				NOT = { has_character_flag = has_personal_coa }
				OR = {
					primary_spouse.dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Dustin
					current_date >= 8283.8.1
				}
			}
			agot_create_personal_coa_for_historical_ai = { ACTOR = character:Ryswell_4 COA_KEY = barbery_dustin_personal_coa }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes

asoiaf_alternative_ages_cleanup_effect = {

	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_focus = education_diplomacy
				has_focus = education_martial
				has_focus = education_stewardship
				has_focus = education_learning
				has_focus = education_intrigue
			}
		}
		set_focus = no
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_sexuality = none
		}
		set_sexuality = heterosexual
	}

	asoiaf_clear_childhood_traits_effect = yes

	if = {
		limit = { is_hostage = yes }
		save_scope_as = hostage
		warden ?= {
			set_variable = {
				name = hook_chance
				value = scope:hostage.bp2_hostage_loyalty_hook_chance_value
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = bp2_hostage_system.0200
				days = 5
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_inactive_trait = not_had_coronation
			has_character_modifier = uncoronated_child_modifier
		}
		remove_character_modifier = uncoronated_child_modifier
		add_character_modifier = uncoronated_modifier
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			any_relation = {
				type = guardian
				count > 0
			}
		}
		random_relation = {
			type = guardian
			save_scope_as = educator
		}
		wrap_up_education_effect = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			any_relation = { type = victim count >= 1 }
		}
		random_relation = { type = victim save_scope_as = victim }
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			set_variable = {
				name = had_childhood_victim_variable
				value = scope:victim
			}
		}
		add_opinion = {
			modifier = childhood_victim
			target = scope:victim
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_relation_potential_rival = scope:victim }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_relation_potential_rival = scope:victim
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_relation = { type = victim count >= 1 }
			}
			remove_relation_victim = scope:victim
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			any_relation = { type = bully count >= 1 }
		}
		random_relation = { type = bully save_scope_as = bully }
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			set_variable = {
				name = had_childhood_bully_variable
				value = scope:bully
			}
		}
		add_opinion = {
			modifier = childhood_bully
			target = scope:bully
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_relation_potential_rival = scope:bully }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_relation_potential_rival = scope:bully
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_relation = { type = bully count >= 1 }
			}
			remove_relation_bully = scope:bully
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				any_relation = { type = crush count >= 1 }
				exists = var:unrequited_crush
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = var:unrequited_crush
			}
			var:unrequited_crush = {
				save_scope_as = crushed_crush
			}
			scope:crushed_crush = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = had_childhood_crush_variable
						value = root
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_relation = { type = crush count >= 1 }
			}
			random_relation = {
				type = crush
				save_scope_as = crush
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = no }
				set_variable = {
					name = had_childhood_crush_variable
					value = scope:crush
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:crush
			}
			save_scope_as = root_crush
			scope:crush = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_relation_crush = root
					}
					add_character_flag = crush_over
					trigger_event = {
						id = childhood.0999
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = var:unrequited_crush
			}
			save_scope_as = root_crush
			scope:crushed_crush = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_relation_crush = root
					}
					add_character_flag = crush_over
					add_character_flag = unrequited_crush
					trigger_event = {
						id = childhood.0999
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:crush
			}
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = childhood_crush
				target = scope:crush
			}
		}
		every_relation = {
			type = crush
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				modifier = childhood_crush
				target = root
			}
			root = {
				remove_relation_crush = prev
			}
		}
	}
}

asoiaf_setup_health_fertility_effect = {
	every_living_character = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				is_human = no
				has_trait = infertile
				has_trait = physique_bad_3
				has_trait = infirm
				has_trait = incapable
				AND = {
					exists = character:asoiaf_Hammer_2
					this = character:asoiaf_Hammer_2
				}
				AND = {
					exists = character:Targaryen_128
					this = character:Targaryen_128
				}
				AND = {
					exists = character:Targaryen_1
					this = character:Targaryen_1
					current_date > 8277.1.1
				}
				AND = {
					exists = character:Targaryen_2
					this = character:Targaryen_2
					current_date > 8277.1.1
				}


				AND = {
					exists = character:Baratheon_2
					this = character:Baratheon_2
					current_date > 8284.1.1
				}
				AND = {
					exists = character:Lannister_129
					this = character:Lannister_129
					exists = character:Lannister_4
				}
				AND = {
					exists = character:Hightower_9
					this = character:Hightower_9
					current_date > 8284.1.1
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = asoiaf_fertility_health_boost
			years = 10
		}
	}
}

asoiaf_new_dnas_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_game_rule = asoiaf_new_dnas_valyrian_rule_on
		}
		character:Targaryen_27 ?= { copy_inheritable_appearance_from = character:Targaryen_asoiaf_27 }
